We can implement an outer loop:
```
def refine_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,alphas, square_tol, max_iters_per, maxfun_per, active_mode):
  best = h, c
  for alpha/active mode:
    while time < deadline:
      c = c_vec(h)
      m = c.max()
      tol = max(1e-8, 0.001*m, 1e-4?)
      active = np.where(c >= m - tol)[0]
      # maybe extend to top K by value if active size < minK or > maxK
      if len(active) < minK: active = top K
      if len(active) > maxK: active = top maxK
      run minimize with active, alpha
      project, eval
      if improved accept and continue; else break to next
```
Need choose tolerances. At optimum, many shifts may be within 1e-4. We can use decreasing tol: 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001. Active max maybe 300. Now, let's design `run_lbfgs_refine` in detail.

```
def calc_c(v,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(v, 1.0-v, mode='full'))*dx)

def c_full(v,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(v, 1.0-v, mode='full')*dx
```

Project via bisection:
```
def project_feasible(y, n):
    S = 0.5*n
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    # handle NaN
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): ...
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - n - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + n + 1.0
    # ensure bracket: s_lo should >=S, s_hi <= S. Adjust.
    def sum_at(lam): return np.clip(y-lam,0,1).sum()
    s_lo = np.clip(y-lo,0,1).sum()
    s_hi = np.clip(y-hi,0,1).sum()
    while s_lo < S and lo > lo-... ?
```
If lo too high? We set lo min-y -n -1: then y-lo >=1+n all? clipped to 1, sum n >=S. hi max-y+n+1: y-hi <= -1-n all ->0. Good. So bracket.
Bisect 60.
Return h. Note this projection minimizes distance to y. It may make h not exactly at bounds. Good.

`active_selector(c,n,tol,max_active,min_active)`:
```
m = c.size
maxv = c.max()
sel = np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
if sel.size < min_active:
   all_idx = np.argsort(c)
   sel = all_idx[-min_active:]
elif sel.size > max_active:
   all_idx = np.argsort(c)
   sel = all_idx[-max_active:]
return sel
```

Gradient:
```
def masked_grad(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
 c = c_full(x,n)
 vals = c[active]
 m = vals.max()
 sh = alpha*(vals-m)
 # if alpha large, underflow; add -sh.max() (0) to be safe
 sh = sh - sh.max()
 w = np.exp(sh)
 sw = w.sum()
 if sw ==0: w = np.ones_like(w)/len
 else: w /= sw
 dx=2/n
 grad = np.zeros(n)
 for wi, idx in zip(w, active):
   if wi < 1e-15: continue
   k = idx - (n-1)
   wm = wi * dx
   if k >=0:
      nv = n-k
      grad[0:nv] += wm*(1.0 - x[k:n])
      grad[k:n] += -wm*x[0:nv]
   else:
      kk=-k; nv=n-kk
      grad[kk:n] += wm*(1.0 - x[0:nv])
      grad[0:nv] += -wm*x[kk:n]
 if mu:
   grad += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
 return grad
```
Need ensure if active is list, `active` array int. If alpha=0? Set alpha>=1.
For α very large and only one max, weights [1,0,...]; grad of max. Good.

For speed, create `shift_info` and pass? Simpler but can optimize.

Outer refine:
```
def active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,tols=(0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001),alphas=(20,100,500),mu=5.0,minact=3,maxact=None,trials-per? ):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=calc_c(h,n)
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    if maxact is None: maxact = max(20, min(400, n//2))
    # Maybe use time budget for each tols/alpha
    for tol in tols:
       for alpha in alphas:
          while time.time() < deadline:
             cvec = c_full(h,n)
             active = select...
             info = make_shift_info(active,n)
             # choose maxiter/maxfun based on n and active size
             maxiter = max(30, min(300, int(deadline? no))) fixed 80?
             maxfun = ...
             t_start=time.time()
             try:
                res = minimize(...)
                new_h = project_feasible(res.x,n)
                new_c = calc_c(new_h,n)
             except:
                break
             if new_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                h = new_h; best_c = new_c; improved=True
             else:
                break # no improvement for this tol/alpha
    return h,best_c
```

We can use exact local search to improve. Design:
- Maintain h and cvec exact (`c_full`).
- Candidate moves: change h_i += d, h_j -= d preserving sum. d can be positive/negative. Bounds. Include binary flips? For pair move with d=1 if h_i=0,h_j=1? This changes sum? +1 and -1 preserves. Or mass transfer from one to another.
- To reduce max, we need pick i,j,d. We can use gradient to choose directions. But maybe random/stochastic local search: maintain a max heap of shifts? For each candidate, update c and if new max < best accept; else revert. Search many small d. This is exact and can polish.
But continuous high-dimensional local search via random pairs might be slow. We can restrict to pairs that appear in active shifts (where c near max) or where gradient suggests.

Given we have exact move update, we can implement a coordinate-like "balanced pair descent":
For each i, choose j maybe in neighborhood. We want decrease maximum c. Since c is sum of local interactions, to reduce high c_r, we need adjust pairs along shifts r. For shift k, c_k = ∑ h_p(1-h_{p+k}). Reducing it: if term high (p one, neighbor zero), can make p smaller or neighbor larger (mass transfer from p to neighbor) but preserves sum. So moves along directions of active shifts. We can generate candidate moves (i,j) where |i-j| in active shifts (or neighbors) and h_i + h_j? With small d.
Actually moving mass from p to q for a term h_p(1-h_q) reduces that term if h_p>0 and h_q<1: exact term change for d>0 from p to q: (p-d)(1-(q+d)) - old = -d(1-q) - d p + d^2? Wait p loses d, q gains d. Term decrease approx d(1-q) + d p - d^2. Good. So for active positive shifts, transfer from 1-side to 0-side along shift reduces that term. But may increase other shifts. A local search can try.

We can define candidate moves:
1. For each shift index in active set (near max), for p=0..n-|k|-1, consider moving d from p to p+k (if k>0) or from p+k to p? Need direction to reduce term: term h[p](1-h[p+k]); if h[p] > h[p+k]? Move mass from larger to smaller: if h[p] > h[p+k], transfer d = min(h[p]-h[p+k], some max) from p to p+k; else reverse? But preserving sum. More generally candidate pair (a,b) with ordered so h[a] > h[b], transfer d = (h[a]-h[b])*β (β=0.1..1) or small fixed.
2. Random pairs among top gradient variable indices.
But evaluating many candidates with O(n) each can be heavy. Maybe use linear approximation to filter: For a candidate move, compute change in active max shifts only using gradient/neighbors, and if likely improves, do exact.

Let's define `apply_pair_move(h, c, i, j, di, dj)`:
Inputs current h, c exact, h values before. Requirements 0≤h_i+di≤1, etc. It will update h[i]+=di; h[j]+=dj (dj usually -di). Then c update:
- `i_first_start = n-1-i`, `i_second_start = i` ? Use in-place:
```
c[n-1-i : n-1-i+n] += di * (1-h_old[0:n])? Wait h values should be old before updates, including for variables i,j. We need old h for neighbor values before applying both. If we do in-place updates sequentially, the linear portion for variable i should use old h of all variables before move, and variable j uses old too. If we apply variable i update using h_new_i? Need use old values. So store vi, vj old. For the slice contributions, for variable i, neighbor h[q] should be old, except if q==j? Cross handled separately. For linear part, if q==j, using old h_j is okay; cross added later. For variable j, using old h_i. So we should compute updates before modifying h, or modify h after. Do:
  vi = h[i]; vj = h[j]
  # store maybe d
  # update c using old h
  c[start1_i:start1_i+n] += di * (1 - h)
  c[start2_i slice reversed] -= di * h
  c[start1_j:start1_j+n] += dj * (1 - h)
  c[start2_j slice reversed] -= dj * h
  # second orders at r0
  r0 = n-1
  c[r0] -= di*di + dj*dj
  # cross
  r1 = n-1 + (j-i)
  r2 = n-1 + (i-j)
  if r1 != r2: c[r1] -= di*dj; c[r2] -= di*dj
  else? If i=j no; else r1 != r2 because j-i ≠ i-j unless 0.
  # now h update
  h[i] = vi + di; h[j] = vj + dj
```
But careful: The linear updates use `h` old, including h_i and h_j values. For variable i first contribution to c at r where q=i (k=0) uses old h_i; second also old h_i. Good. For variable i contribution to r where q=j uses old h_j. Cross added. For variable j contribution to same r uses old h_i. Good. Since we haven't modified h yet, correct.
However, the slice updates as written `c[s:s+n] += di * (1 - h)` will also update c at all r, including r where neighbor is i/j. That's intended old neighbor values. The cross term corrects product of deltas.
What about the fact that variable i's own value appears in neighbor array for some other shift where q? For k? Linear derivative computed using old h_i. Good.
But if we update c for variable i and variable j, and their affected slices overlap (they do at many r), we add both linear contributions. Correct as linearization. Cross only where both in same term.

Let's rederive variable linear update using index mapping.
For c_r = ∑_{p} h_p (1 - h_{p+k}), k = r-(n-1).
For variable i as first (p=i): valid if q=i+k in [0,n). For each q in [0,n), k=q-i, r = n-1 + q - i. Contribution to derivative wrt h_i: (1 - h_q) * δ. So first update indices r_first(q)=n-1 + q - i for q=0..n-1. We used start n-1-i length n, correct. Contribution δ*(1-old_h_q).
For variable i as second (q=i): p = i-k, k = i-p, r = n-1 + i - p for p=0..n-1. Derivative: h_p * derivative of (1-h_i) = -h_p δ. Contribution -δ*old_h_p to r_second(p)=n-1+i-p. For p=0..n-1, r descends from n+i-1 to i. We did `c[i:n+i][::-1] += -δ*h`. For n=2 i=0, second indices p=0 r=1, p=1 r=0. `c[0:2][::-1]` corresponds [r=1,r=0] and h[0],h[1] -> correct. For var0 δ=0.1: first +0.1*(1-h) to r q=0 r=1, q=1 r=2 -> c[1]+.05, c[2]+.05. second -0.1*h to r p=0 r=1, p=1 r=0 -> c[1]-.05, c[0]-.05. Net var0: c[0]-.05,c[1]0,c[2]+.05. Var1 δ=-0.1: first r q=0 r=0, q=1 r=1 -> c[0]-.05,c[1]-.05. second p=0 r=2, p=1 r=1 -> contribution -δ*h = +.1*h to c[2],c[1]: c[2]+.05,c[1]+.05. Net linear: c0=-.10, c1=0, c2=0. So c [.15,.5,.25]. Cross/self: old c [.25,.5,.25] -> [.15,.5,.25]; second order at r0 (k=0) subtract di^2+dj^2: c1 -.02 ->.48; cross at r1=2 add a^2 .01 ->.36, r2=0 add .01 ->.16. Final [.16,.48,.36]. Correct! Our earlier calculation mishandled var1 second sign. The update formula is correct if implemented as `c[i:n+i][::-1] -= delta * h`? For var1 delta=-0.1: -= (-0.1)*h => +=0.1*h, as intended. Good.

Thus exact pair move O(2n) slice operations with 4 slices (2 per variable) plus few scalar updates. Very fast.

We can use this for local search. Important: When applying inverse to reject, use di'=-di, dj'=-dj. But the cross/self terms are quadratic and sign? If we apply inverse update starting from new state, it should restore old c exactly. Let's test: Since update is exact Taylor with quadratic complete for the move (because c is quadratic in moved variables). Applying inverse using new h? The formula assumes old h before inverse is new h, deltas negative. It should revert because quadratic exact. But numerator: self subtraction uses di^2 (same) which would subtract again, not add. If we call apply_move with -di on new state, self term `-(-di)^2` subtracts diamond again, wrong. Because the exact update is not linear + delta^2 with sign? Wait change from old to new includes -di^2 for self. To revert from new to old, the change is old - new, delta'=-di. The exact change from new to old should also have second order - (delta')^2? Let's compute single term h(1-h): old->new with +d: Δ = d(1-2h_old) - d^2. New->old with -d: new value h+d. Δ' = -d(1-2h_new) - d^2? Expand: (h)(1-h) - (h+d)(1-h-d) = -d(1-2h-d)?? Let's compute: old T0=h-h^2, new T1=(h+d)(1-h-d)=h-h^2 + d(1-2h)-d^2. Reverse Δ'=T0-T1 = -d(1-2h)+d^2. In terms of h_new=h+d: derivative of T at new: 1-2h_new=1-2h-2d. Linear for -d: -d(1-2h_new)= -d +2dh+2d^2. Add second? - (delta')^2 = -d^2 gives -d +2dh + d^2, matches Δ' = -d +2dh +d^2. Good. So the same formula with current h_new and delta'=-d is correct: self term `-delta'^2` indeed subtracts d^2? Wait Δ' = linear + (-delta'^2)? In formula Δ = d(1-2h_current) - d^2 with h_current old for forward. For reverse, h_current=new, delta'=-d: linear = -d(1-2h_new), second = -d^2? We found Δ' = -d(1-2h_old)+d^2. Are these equal? h_new=h_old+d: -d(1-2h_old-2d)-d^2 = -d+2dh_old+2d^2-d^2 = -d+2dh_old+d^2. Yes. So self term is always -delta^2 (negative relative to current). Forward self Δ included -d^2; reverse also -d^2. It should restore because linear part differs. Good. Cross term is `-di*dj` with deltas from current to next. For reverse, delta'=-di,-dj, product same (positive? (-di)(-dj)=di*dj), so cross term `-di*dj` same sign. Does that restore? Let's test pair move n=2: start old c, apply forward gave new. Apply reverse with delta'=-.1,+.1 on new h using current h_new. Formula cross subtract `di'*dj' = (-.1)(.1)=-.01`, so -di'dj'=+0.01 again? That would not restore if forward added +0.01; reverse should subtract +0.01. Wait cross term sign for reverse: exact term x(1-y) forward change with dx=d, dy=-d: Δ=linear - d*(-d)=linear + d^2 (cross +a^2). Reverse from new to old with dx'=-d, dy'=+d: exact change = -d? Formula with current and deltas dx'=-d,dy'=+d: linear_current = dx'(1-y') - dy' x' = -d(1-y+d) + d(x+d) = -d +dy -d^2 +dx d + d^2? Let's symbolic: = -d(1-y+d) + d(x+d) = -d+dy -d^2 + dx d +d^2? Cross -dx*dy = -(-a*a)=+a^2. Total = -a(1-y0)-a x0 -a^2 = -forward total. Good. Cross term formula always `-dx*dy`; for reverse dx*dy=-a^2 -> +a^2, but linear is more negative by 2a^2 compared to forward, restoring. So calling apply_move with inverse deltas using current h works. Good.

How to use local search.
We can maintain `cvec` for current h. For candidate move, apply, get max = cvec.max()*dx? We can keep c raw and compare to best raw max (c*? dx constant). Since dx constant per n, compare raw. But cvec values are raw sums; objective = max*c dx. We can track `best_raw = np.max(cvec)`. Then c5=best_raw*dx.
Candidate move d. Need ensure bounds. For mass transfer preserving sum, choose pair i,j and amount a>0 from i to j (h_i -= a, h_j += a) or vice versa. We can bound a by h_i (if i loses) and 1-h_j (if j gains).
We can generate moves that reduce highest correlations. But random moves with exact update can be used as stochastic hill climbing. Maybe use simulated annealing? We only accept decreases. Could get stuck; but L-BFGS before.

Candidate generation strategies:
- Use current max shifts: active = top shifts with c_raw > best_raw - tol*best.
- For each active shift k, terms with high contribution can be reduced by moving mass from p with high h_p and q with low h_q. We can sample top contributions for that shift: term values = h[p]*(1-h[q]) (for k positive; for negative analogous). We can compute `term = h[:n-|k|] * (1 - h[|k|:])` for k>=0. Or for k<0: h[|k|:]* (1-h[:n-|k|])? But term same pairs with orientation; reducing pair by equalizing. Candidate move from higher to lower among pair. For a shift k, to reduce c_k, transfer between p and q = p+k. If h[p] > h[q], move from p to q; else opposite? Actually term h[p](1-h[q]) is not symmetric; if h[p] low and h[q] high, term small; moving from q to p may increase term? Term p->q orientation fixed. Full objective includes both k and -k; pair appears in two shifts with reversed orientation. The sum of both terms = a(1-b)+b(1-a) = a+b-2ab, symmetric, minimized by making a,b close or both extremes? For a+b≈1, sum=1-2ab, minimized when ab max (a=b=0.5) if sum=1; if both 0 or1 sum=0? But area constraints. Moving mass from high to low equalizes and reduces sum of both orientations if a+b between? Derivative of a(1-b)+b(1-a) =1-2ab; if moving from high a to low b: a decreases, b increases; ab increases if a>0.5? For a+b=1, ab max at 0.5. If a=0.8,b=0.2, moving to 0.6/0.4 reduces sum from 0.48 to 0.36. If a=0.6,b=0.2 sum? not fixed. So equalizing high/low often reduces both. Candidate: for pair connected by active shift, transfer a fraction of difference.
- We can sample such pairs from active shifts with largest term values. For each top shift, compute term arrays length L, find top pairs, construct moves.

Implementation details for `try_candidate`:
```
def improve_candidate(h,n,deadline):
  h = project_feasible(h,n)
  c = c_full(h,n); best_raw = c.max(); bounds=...
  # L-BFGS phase: while time before deadline*0.6 maybe
  lb_deadline = min(time.time()+? , deadline-80)
  # We can call active_lbfgs with deadline=lb_deadline
  h, best_raw = active_lbfgs(h,n,lb_deadline)
  # Recompute c
  cvec = c_full(h,n)
  # Local exact phase until deadline
  h, new_raw = local_exact_search(h,cvec,n,deadline)
  # final project and eval
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  c=calc_c(h,n)
  return h,c
```

Pseudo:
```
def active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=calc_c(h,n)
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  tols=[0.005,0.0025,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,1e-5]
  alphas=[20,80,400,2000]
  maxact=max(30,min(400,n//2))
  minact=3
  for pass in range(4):
    if time.time() > deadline: break
    for tol in tols:
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
        cvec = compute_c_vec(h,n)
        active = choose_active(cvec,n,tol,minact,maxact)
        if active.size==0: break
        info = (active, active-(n-1))
        # maybe choose maxiter/fun
        maxiter,maxfun = lbfgs_limits(n, active.size)
        # if active.size small, can allow more? maybe
        try:
           res = minimize(mask_obj, h, jac=mask_grad, args=(n, active, active-(n-1), alpha, mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':8,'maxfun':maxfun})
           new_h = project_feasible(res.x,n)
           new_c = calc_c(new_h,n)
        except Exception:
           break
        if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
           h=new_h; best_c=new_c
           # repeat this tol/alpha at most? Use while? We can just continue to next to avoid long. Maybe run one more immediately:
        else:
           break
        if time.time() > deadline: break
  return h,best_c
```
But this will do one L-BFGS per tol/alpha then break after improvement or not. If improvement, it moves to next tol/alpha, maybe more difficult. That's okay. Implement `apply_move` carefully with in-place views. Need ensure `c` is a writable numpy array not sharing? `c_full` returns new. Good.
```
def apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,di,dj):
    n = len(h)
    # no check bounds; caller ensures
    # variable i
    s1 = n - 1 - i
    c[s1:s1+n] += di * (1.0 - h)
    # variable j
    s1j = n - 1 - j
    c[s1j:s1j+n] += dj * (1.0 - h)
    # second factor: c[i:i+n] reversed -= di*h ; c[j:j+n] reversed -= dj*h
    # Need be careful if i+n > 2n? i max n-1 => i+n=2n-1 ok length n ends at 2n-2.
    c[i:i+n][::-1] -= di * h
    c[j:j+n][::-1] -= dj * h
    # self and cross:
    r0 = n - 1
    c[r0] -= di*di + dj*dj
    r1 = n - 1 + (j - i)
    r2 = n - 1 + (i - j)
    cross = -di * dj
    if r1 == r2:
       c[r1] += cross
    else:
       c[r1] += cross
       c[r2] += cross
```
But wait: We update c for variable i and j using old h. If we do both first contributions before second? All use `h` old (not updated yet). Good. However, when i and j differ, the slice `c[s1_i:s1_i+n]` and `c[s1_j:s1_j+n]` may overlap; adding sequential is fine (linear sum). But the second factor slices also overlap. Good.
Potential subtlety: The linear update for variable i should use h_old for all q, including q=j. We do before h update. Good.
But when we add both variable updates, for a term where i and j are both in same term, the linear part from variable i uses old h_j, and from variable j uses old h_i. Cross added. Good.
Need ensure if `i` and `j` are same? We won't call; if same, formula would double? skip.
Bounds check: `ni = h[i]+di`, `nj=h[j]+dj`; due floating maybe. For transfer source s, dest t, di=-d for source, dj=+d for dest. Require 0<=. Use small epsilon.
When rejecting, call with di,dj opposite? If forward was i=s di=-d, j=t dj=+d. Reverse should di=+d, dj=-d and use current h. We'll call `apply_pair_move(h,c,s,t,d,-d)`. This should restore but due numerical maybe drift. Recompute periodically.

Candidate interpolation:
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
   n_old=len(h)
   if n_old==1: return np.full(n_new, h[0])? not.
   x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
   x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
   h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
   return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)
```
If downsample, same. This linear interpolation preserves area approximately; project fixes.

Threshold:
```
def threshold_project(h,n,noise=0.0,seed=None):
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   m = int(round(n/2)) # if n even
   if m <=0 or m>=n: return h
   if noise>0 or seed is not None: rng add small.
   # choose top m
   order = np.argsort(-h, kind='stable')[:m]
   out = np.zeros(n)
   out[order] = 1.0
   # project not needed sum exactly m
   return out
```
If n odd, m target not integer; use project. But target n even.

Random binary:
```
def random_binary(n, seed):
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 m=n//2
 idx=rng.choice(n,m,replace=False)
 h=zeros; h[idx]=1
 return h
```

```
def random_smooth_start(n, seed):
 rng=...
 x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
 y=0.5
 for f in rng.integers(1,10,size=6):
   y += rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/2.0 + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
 y=0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(y)
 return project_feasible(y,n)
```

`improve_candidate` pseudocode:

```
def improve_candidate(h, n, deadline):
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   best_c = c5(h,n)
   # split deadline into chunks, maybe 3 cycles
   chunk_count = 0
   while time.time() < deadline and chunk_count < 4:
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 25: break
       # L-BFGS chunk
       lb_time = min(rem * 0.45, 120.0)
       if rem < 80: lb_time = min(rem*0.3, 40.0)
       lb_deadline = min(deadline - 20, time.time() + max(10, lb_time))
       if lb_deadline > time.time() + 5:
           h = active_lbfgs(h,n,lb_deadline)  # returns h,bestc but ignore c? recompute
       # local chunk
       local_time = min(rem*0.5, 150.0)
       local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(15, local_time))
       cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
       h = local_exact_search(h, cvec, n, local_deadline)
       c = c5(h,n)
       if c < best_c: best_c = c
       chunk_count += 1
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   c = c5(h,n)
   if c >= best_c? We should return best exact h, not current if worse. Keep best_h inside:
   update when c<best. Return best_h.
```

```
    if n <= 400: return (300, 8000)
    if n <= 700: return (250, 4000)
    if n <= 1000: return (180, 1600)
    if n <= 1400: return (130, 800)
```

```
def active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   target=0.5*n
   tols=[...]
   alphas=[...]
   mu=10.0
   maxact=...
   pass_idx=0
   while pass_idx<3 and time.time()<deadline:
      for tol in tols:
         if time.time()>deadline-2: break
         for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time()>deadline-2: break
            cvec=compute_c_vec(h,n)
            active=choose_active(cvec,n,tol,5,maxact)
            if active.size==0: break
            ks=active-(n-1)
            maxiter,maxfun=lbfgs_limits(n,active.size)
            # maybe if active.size==1 and alpha>100 skip? no
            t_run=time.time()
            try:
               res=minimize(mask_obj,h,jac=mask_grad,args=(n,active,ks,alpha,mu,target),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':8,'maxfun':maxfun})
               new_h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
               new_c=compute_c5(new_h,n)
            except Exception:
               break
            if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
               h=new_h; best_c=new_c
               # repeat same up to 2 if time
               repeat=1
               while repeat<3 and time.time()<deadline-2:
                  cvec=compute_c_vec(h,n); active=choose_active(...); ks=...
                  try run; if accept repeat++ else break
            else:
               # no improvement this alpha; try smaller? break to next alpha? maybe if alpha high no improve, lower alpha might; alphas increasing, if high no, lower maybe? We iterate increasing; if no improve at 10, try 50? yes break? Better continue to next alpha after a no-improve? Could. But many no-improve runs waste. Do: if no improve and alpha>10, break to next tol? hmm.
```

```
def generate_local_candidates(h,cvec,n):
    m=cvec.size
    A=min(24,m)
    # top_idx by raw c
    if m <= A: top=np.arange(m)
    else: top=np.argpartition(cvec, -A)[-A:]
    top=top[np.argsort(-cvec[top])]
    seen=set(); cands=[]
    P=10
    for idx in top[:min(18,A)]:
       k=int(idx-(n-1))
       if k >= 0:
          L=n-k
          if L<2: continue
          a=h[:L]; b=h[k:k+L]
       else:
          kk=-k; L=n-kk
          if L<2: continue
          a=h[kk:n]; b=h[:L]
       total = a + b - 2.0*a*b
       diff = np.abs(a-b)
       score = total * diff
       # Maybe cap L if large
       P_use = min(P, L)
       if P_use <=0 or L <= P_use:
          idxs = np.argsort(-score)
       else:
          idxs = np.argpartition(-score, P_use)[:P_use]
       for p in idxs:
          p=int(p)
          if k>=0:
             ai=float(a[p]); bi=float(b[p]); si=p; ti=k+p
          else:
             ai=float(a[p]); bi=float(b[p]); si=kk+p; ti=p
          d=abs(ai-bi)
          if d < 0.05: continue  # maybe skip tiny
          # If source is lower? We set s higher already
          if ai > bi: s=si; t=ti; dd=d
          else: s=ti; t=si; dd=d
          key = s*n + t
          if key in seen: continue
          seen.add(key)
          cands.append((s,t,dd))
          if len(cands) >= 250: break
       if len(cands)>=250: break
    cands.sort(key=candidate_score, reverse=True)
    return cands
```

Detailed `local_exact_search`:
```
def local_exact_search(h_in, cvec_in, n, deadline):
    h = h_in.copy()
    cvec = cvec_in.copy()
    safe_h = h.copy()
    safe_cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)  # exact start
    safe_raw = float(safe_cvec.max())
    cvec = safe_cvec.copy()
    best_raw = safe_raw
    acc_since = 0
    rounds = 0
    max_rounds = 10000
    while time.time() < deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
        rounds += 1
        if acc_since >= 12 or rounds % 25 == 0:
            cvec_check = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
            raw_check = float(cvec_check.max())
            if raw_check < safe_raw - 1e-13:
                safe_raw = raw_check
                safe_h = h.copy()
                safe_cvec = cvec_check.copy()
                cvec = cvec_check
                best_raw = safe_raw
                acc_since = 0
            else:
                h = safe_h.copy()
                cvec = safe_cvec.copy()
                best_raw = safe_raw
                acc_since = 0
            continue
        cands = generate_local_candidates(h,cvec,n)
        if not cands:
            break
        round_improved = False
        for (s,t,dd) in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            if s==t: continue
            if h[s] <= 1e-12 or h[s] >= 1-1e-12? source could be 1? moving from 1 okay; need h[s]>0, h[t]<1.
            max_d = min(h[s], 1.0-h[t])
            if max_d <= 1e-12: continue
            # try fractions
            for frac in (0.5,0.25,1.0,0.1):
                d = frac*dd
                if d > max_d: d = max_d
                if d <= 1e-12: continue
                # forward move from s to t
                apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,-d,d)
                new_raw = float(cvec.max())
                if new_raw < best_raw - 1e-12:
                    best_raw = new_raw
                    acc_since += 1
                    round_improved = True
                    # keep move; break fractions and round to regenerate
                    break
                else:
                    apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,d,-d)
            if round_improved:
                break
        if not round_improved:
            # no improvement; break
            break
    # final ensure safe best
    final_h = safe_h.copy()
    final_cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(final_h,n)
    final_raw = float(final_cvec.max())
    return final_h, final_cvec, final_raw
```

Thus `apply_pair_move`:
```
def apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,di,dj):
   n=len(h)
   one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
   h_rev = h[::-1]
   c[n-1-i : n-1-i+n] += di * one_minus_h
   c[n-1-j : n-1-j+n] += dj * one_minus_h
   c[i : i+n] -= di * h_rev
   c[j : j+n] -= dj * h_rev
   r0 = n-1
   c[r0] -= di*di + dj*dj
   r1 = n-1 + (j-i)
   r2 = n-1 + (i-j)
   cross = -di*dj
   if r1 == r2:
       c[r1] += cross
   else:
       c[r1] += cross
       c[r2] += cross
```
Wait first contribution uses `one_minus_h` for all q. For variable i first, q ranges 0..n-1, yes h array. Good.
But we use `one_minus_h` old. We haven't updated h yet. Good.
Now, cross indices r1/r2 are within 0..2n-2? j-i range -(n-1)..n-1, plus n-1 range 0..2n-2. Good.
For k=0 self: r0=n-1. If i and j distinct, self terms for both. If i and j are separated? Yes.
What about if i and j are same? no.
If d move source to dest: source di=-d, dest dj=+d. Cross `cross = -(-d)*(d)=d^2`, positive. Good.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
   t_start=time.time()
   deadline = t_start + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
   # get initial
   try:
      h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
   except Exception:
      h0 = None
   if h0 is not None and h0.size:
      n0=int(h0.size); h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
      best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h0,n0); best_n=n0
   else:
      n0=500; h0=random_smooth(n0,seed); ...
   # quick improve parent if n0 <= 800?
   if time.time() < deadline - 60:
      # allocate min(90, deadline-60)
      dl = min(deadline - 40, time.time() + 80.0)
      h_try = improve_candidate(h0,n0,dl)
      if h_try[1] < best_c: update
   # Choose resolutions
   # We'll attempt resolutions in list based on current best_n maybe >500.
   resolutions=[]
   base=best_n
   for n in [500,800,1000,1200,1500]:
       if n not in seen and n>=? maybe if n>best_n or best_n not in list? add
   # Ensure if best_n >1500? add best_n maybe.
   # Sort? Start low to high.
   for n in resolutions:
      if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
      # create start from best_h (global best so far) resampled to n
      start = resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
      # allocate budget based on n and remaining
      rem = deadline - time.time()
      if rem < 90: break
      # Maybe main high allocations: if n==1000/1200 more
      alloc = min(rem - 40, get_alloc(n, rem))
      if alloc < 60: alloc=min(rem,60)
      dl = time.time()+alloc
      h_try,c_try=improve_candidate(start,n,dl)
      if c_try < best_c: best_h,h_try; best_n=n
      # After improving at n, maybe create threshold start at same n if time

          # maybe smooth threshold? if pure binary L-BFGS can handle.
          alloc2=min(rem? 80, deadline-40)

   # Final: try downsample best to lower n if time

      update
      start=random_binary(best_n,seed)
      h_try,c_try=improve_candidate(start,best_n,deadline-2)
   final project best_h, best_c=compute_c5
   return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def process_tasks(tasks):
  for name,n,alloc in tasks:
    if time.time() > deadline - 45: break
    rem=deadline - time.time()
    alloc_eff = min(alloc, max(20, rem - 30))
    if alloc_eff < 25: break
    if name=='main': start=resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
    elif name=='threshold': start=threshold_project(resample_h(best_h,best_n,n),n,seed=...)
    elif name=='random': start=random_smooth(n,seed+..)
    else if name=='binary': start=random_binary...
    dl=time.time()+alloc_eff
    h_try,c_try=improve_candidate(start,n,dl)
    update
```

`improve_candidate` code:
```
def improve_candidate(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    loop=0
    while time.time() < deadline and loop < 4:
        loop += 1
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 20: break
        # L-FP?
        if rem > 40:
            lb_frac = 0.45 if loop == 1 else 0.35
            lb_alloc = min(rem * lb_frac, 120.0 if n <= 800 else 90.0)
            lb_alloc = max(20.0, lb_alloc)
            lb_dl = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + lb_alloc)
            if lb_dl > time.time() + 5.0:
                h, c_tmp = active_lbfgs(h,n,lb_dl)
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                c_tmp = compute_c5(h,n)
                if c_tmp < best_c:
                    best_c=c_tmp; best_h=h.copy()
        # local chunk
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 15:
            loc_alloc = min(rem * 0.75, 180.0) # but leave a bit for next loop
            if deadline - time.time() < 60: loc_alloc = deadline - time.time() - 2
            loc_dl = min(deadline - 2, time.time() + max(10, loc_alloc))
            cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
            h, cvec_raw, raw = local_exact_search(h,cvec,n,loc_dl)
            h = project_feasible(h,n)
            c_tmp = compute_c5(h,n)
            if c_tmp < best_c:
                best_c=c_tmp; best_h=h.copy()
            # If local took long, maybe break
    final project best_h, eval
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
   ...

   passes=0

     passes+=1; stalls=0? maybe global stalls

         run one L-BFGS
```

Implementation of bisection:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    target=n*0.5
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    if y.size != n: y=y.resize? no.
    if not all finite: y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - n - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + n + 1.0
    # ensure sum(lo side) >= target and hi side <= target
    for _ in range(10):
       s_lo = np.clip(y-lo,0,1).sum()
       if s_lo >= target: break
       lo -= n
    for _ in range(10):
       s_hi = np.clip(y-hi,0,1).sum()
       if s_hi <= target: break
       hi += n
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    return np.clip(y-lam,0,1)
```
If target non-integer for odd n, bisection gives fractional at one variable. Good.
But using `n+1` in bound might be overly wide but okay.

`resample_h`: If `n_old` and `n_new` both even, linear interpolation may produce slight area error. Project.
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
   n_old=len(h)
   if n_old == n_new: return project_feasible(h,n_new)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
   h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
   return project_feasible(h_new,n_new)
```

`threshold_project`: If n even, m=n//2. If n odd, use top floor and one fractional? But we use even. Implement robust:
```
def threshold_project(h,n,seed=None):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   target=n*0.5
   if abs(target-round(target))<1e-12:
      m=int(round(target))
      if 0<m<n:
         order=np.argsort(-h,kind='stable')[:m]
         out=np.zeros(n); out[order]=1.0; return out
   else:
      m=int(floor(target))
      out=zeros; order top m+1? set one fractional target-m
   return h
```
No need rng. Maybe add tiny noise parameter? Could use seed to jitter to avoid ties. But top parent deterministic. For diversity, threshold of different resampled best enough.

`random_smooth_start`:
```
def random_smooth_start(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    y=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(8):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,11))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.7))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       y += amp*np.cos(np.pi*f*x + ph)  # f half-period over [0,2]? period 2/f
    y = 0.5 + 0.45*np.tanh(y)
    y = np.clip(y,0,1)
    return project_feasible(y,n)
```


`choose_active`:
```
def choose_active(c,n,tol,minact,maxact):
   maxv = float(c.max())
   thresh = maxv - tol
   idx = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
   m=idx.size
   if m < minact:
      if c.size <= minact: return np.arange(c.size)
      idx = np.argpartition(c, -minact)[-minact:]
   elif m > maxact:
      if c.size <= maxact: return np.arange(c.size)
      idx = np.argpartition(c, -maxact)[-maxact:]
   # sort descending maybe not needed
   return idx.astype(int, copy=False)
```
If tol very small and m=0? maxv≥maxv so at least one. Good.

`lbfgs_limits` returns maxiter, maxfun. If active.size large, maybe reduce.
```
def lbfgs_limits(n, actlen):
   if n <= 500:
      mi, mf = 250, 3000
   elif n <= 800: mi,mf=200,1800
   elif n<=1100: mi,mf=160,1000
   else: mi,mf=110,500
   if actlen <= 25: return (mi*2, min(mf*3, 8000))
   if actlen <= 60: return (mi*2, mf*2)
   return (mi,mf)
```
This may allow long runs for small active. Good.

```
def compute_c_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*dx
```

Threshold:
```
def threshold_project(h,n,seed=None):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   target=0.5*n
   m=int(round(target))
   if abs(target-m)<1e-12 and 0<m<n:
      out=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
      # stable argsort
      order=np.argsort(-h, kind='stable')[:m]
      out[order]=1.0
      return out
   return h
```

But in objective, if sw=0, log(sw) invalid. Use close. We can compute weights in a helper `softmax_weights(vals, alpha)` top-level to reuse in grad. Good.
```
def softmax_weights(vals, alpha):
   if vals.size == 0: return np.zeros(0)
   m = float(vals.max())
   if alpha <= 0: return np.ones(vals.size)/vals.size
   sh = alpha*(vals-m)
   sh = sh - sh.max() # 0
   w = np.exp(sh)
   sw = float(w.sum())
   if sw > 1e-300:
      return w/sw
   tol = max(1e-12, 20.0/alpha)
   close = vals >= m - tol
   cnt = float(np.count_nonzero(close))
   if cnt <=0:
      # pick top 1
      idx = int(np.argmax(vals)); w = np.zeros(vals.size); w[idx]=1; return w
   w = np.zeros(vals.size); w[close]=1.0/cnt; return w
```
Objective needs logsumexp. Could compute in obj directly. Duplicate weights? We can compute weights and approximate lse? If using fallback, lse maybe log(cnt) if close. Better:

```
def softmax_obj_part(vals, alpha):
  m=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-m); smx=sh.max(); exp=np.exp(sh-smx); sw=exp.sum(); lse=smx+log(sw); return m + lse/alpha, weights? 
```

`mask_grad`:
```
dx=2.0/n
grad=np.zeros(n)
for pos in range(active.size):
  wi = w[pos]
  if wi < 1e-15: continue
  kk = int(ks[pos])
  wm = wi*dx
  if kk >=0:
     nv = n-kk
     grad[:nv] += wm*(1.0-x[kk:n])
     grad[kk:] += -wm*x[:nv]
  else:
     kk = -kk; nv=n-kk
     grad[kk:] += wm*(1.0-x[:nv])
     grad[:nv] += -wm*x[kk:]
if mu:
   grad += 2.0*mu*(x.sum()-target)
return grad
```

`active_lbfgs`:
```
def active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] # list comprehension okay (not lambda/closure)
   target=0.5*n
   tols=(0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,5e-5)
   alphas=(10.0,50.0,250.0,1000.0)
   mu=10.0
   minact=5
   maxact=max(20, min(220, n//4 if n>=80 else 20))
   stalls=0
   passes=0
   while passes<3 and time.time() < deadline:
      passes +=1
      for tol in tols:
        if time.time()>deadline-2: break
        if stalls>=4: break
        for alpha in alphas:
          if time.time()>deadline-2: break
          cvec=compute_c_vec(h,n)
          active=choose_active(cvec,n,tol,minact,maxact)
          if active.size==0: break
          ks=active-(n-1)
          maxiter,maxfun=lbfgs_limits(n,active.size)
          try:
             res=minimize(mask_obj,h,jac=mask_grad,args=(n,active,ks,alpha,mu,target),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':8,'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxcor':10})
             new_h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
             new_c=compute_c5(new_h,n)
          except Exception:
             stalls +=1
             continue
          if new_c < best_c - 1e-13:
             h=new_h; best_c=new_c; stalls=0
          else:
             stalls +=1
          if stalls>=4: break
      if stalls>=4: break
   return h,best_c
```
If a run no-improve due numerical but new_c slightly better 1e-14; not accept. stalls may break. okay.
Could `minimize` with bounds and `maxfun` raise if not converge; returns. Good.

Potential conflict: `bounds` list reused across runs; L-BFGS doesn't modify. okay.

Pseudo run code:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+max(20.0,budget-6.0)
    # initial
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       h0=None
    if h0 is None or h0.size==0:
       n0=500
       h0=random_smooth_start(n0,seed)
    else:
       n0=int(h0.size)
       h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0.copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n0)
    best_n=n0
    # short early refine parent, but improve_candidate may use a lot. If budget small, skip.
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       dl = min(deadline - 20, time.time() + 60.0) # maybe 60 if budget>=150 else remaining
       if budget < 150: dl = deadline - 2
       h_try,c_try = improve_candidate(best_h,best_n,dl)
       if c_try < best_c:
           best_c=c_try; best_h=h_try.copy(); best_n=best_n
    # tasks
    target_ns = []
    for n in [600, 800, 1000, 1100, 1300, 1500]:
       # maybe skip if too close to best_n? We want different resolutions.
    # Determine allocations as fractions of remaining? Use a table.
    tasks = []
    tasks.append((600, 70.0, 'main'))
    tasks.append((900, 120.0, 'main'))
    tasks.append((1100, 240.0, 'main'))
    tasks.append((1400, 160.0, 'main'))
    # threshold/random main at 1100/1400
    tasks.append((1100, 80.0, 'threshold'))
    tasks.append((1400, 50.0, 'threshold'))
    tasks.append((best_n, 40.0, 'random'))
    # If best_n not in high, maybe task 1000? Use fixed.
    for (n,alloc,kind) in tasks:
       if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 45: break
       # scale alloc if rem smaller
       alloc_eff = min(alloc, rem - 20.0)
       if alloc_eff < 30.0:
          alloc_eff = min(rem, 30.0)
          if alloc_eff < 15: break
       if kind == 'main':
          start_h = resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
       elif kind == 'threshold':
          base = resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
          start_h = threshold_project(base,n,int(seed+n))
       else: # random
          start_h = random_smooth_start(n,int(seed*1000+n))
       dl = time.time() + alloc_eff
       if dl > deadline: dl = deadline
       h_try,c_try = improve_candidate(start_h,n,dl)
       if c_try < best_c:
          best_c=c_try; best_h=h_try.copy(); best_n=int(n)
    # final local/exact on best
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
       cvec=compute_c_vec_raw(best_h,best_n)
       h_try,c_try=local_exact_search(best_h,cvec,best_n,deadline-1)
       h_try=project_feasible(h_try,best_n)
       c_try=compute_c5(h_try,best_n)
       if c_try < best_c:
          best_c=c_try; best_h=h_try.copy()
    # final project and eval
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

```
def improve_candidate(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    cycles=0
    while time.time() < deadline and cycles < 5:
       cycles +=1
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 18: break
       if rem > 45:
          if n <= 700: lb_cap=120.0
          elif n <= 1000: lb_cap=100.0
          else: lb_cap=80.0
          lb_frac = 0.45 if cycles==1 else 0.35
          lb_alloc = min(rem*lb_frac, lb_cap)
          lb_alloc = max(15.0, lb_alloc)
          lb_dl = min(deadline - 12.0, time.time()+lb_alloc)
          if lb_dl > time.time()+4.0:
              h, c_tmp = active_lbfgs(h,n,lb_dl)
              h=project_feasible(h,n); c_tmp=compute_c5(h,n)
              if c_tmp < best_c: best_c=c_tmp; best_h=h.copy()
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 12: break
       loc_alloc = min(rem*0.7, 180.0)
       if rem < 45: loc_alloc = rem - 2.0
       loc_alloc = max(8.0, loc_alloc)
       loc_dl = min(deadline - 1.0, time.time()+loc_alloc)
       cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
       h, c_tmp = local_exact_search(h,cvec,n,loc_dl)
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c_tmp=compute_c5(h,n)
       if c_tmp < best_c: best_c=c_tmp; best_h=h.copy()
       # If local improved a lot, maybe another cycle; if not, maybe next cycle L-BFGS? Loop continues.
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def local_exact_search(h, cvec, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(best_h,n)
    best_raw = float(cvec.max())
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if cvec.max() > best_raw? recompute? We keep cvec exact for h? At start h=best_h. After no improvement, break.
       cands = generate_local_candidates(h,cvec,n)
       if not cands: break
       round_improved=False
       for (s,t,dd) in cands:
          if time.time()>=deadline: break
          old_h = h  # no copy
          # To revert if verification fails, need apply inverse from current moved h. We can apply forward, if fail inverse. If verification passes, set h=best new.
          max_d = min(h[s],1-h[t])
          if max_d <=1e-12: continue
          for frac in (0.5,0.25,1.0,0.1):
             d = min(frac*dd, max_d)
             if d<=1e-12: continue
             apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,-d,d)
             new_raw = float(cvec.max())
             if new_raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                 # exact verify
                 cvec_check = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
                 raw_check = float(cvec_check.max())
                 if raw_check < best_raw - 1e-10:
                     best_h = h.copy()
                     best_c = raw_check * (2.0/n)
                     best_raw = raw_check
                     cvec = cvec_check  # exact for best_h
                     round_improved=True
                     break
                 else:
                     # revert move
                     apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,d,-d)
                     # cvec approximate old; we can recompute? maybe not; but to be safe, cvec=compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
                     cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
             else:
                 apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,d,-d)
             if round_improved: break
          if round_improved: break
       if not round_improved:
          break
    # final project best_h
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```
This guarantees h always equals best_h at round start. If exact verification fails, h returned to old best. Good.

Let's implement `local_exact_search` with this exact verification. Simpler and safe.

```
def local_exact_search(h,cvec,n,deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(best_h,n)
    best_raw = float(cvec.max())
    best_c = best_raw*dx
    actions=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        cands = generate_local_candidates(h,cvec,n)
        if not cands: break
        improved=False
        actions=0
        for cand in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            s,t,dd = cand
            if s==t: continue
            hs = float(h[s]); ht=float(h[t])
            max_d = min(hs, 1.0-ht)
            if max_d <= 1e-12: continue
            for frac in (0.5,0.25,1.0,0.1):
                d = frac*dd
                if d > max_d: d = max_d
                if d <= 1e-12: continue
                apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,-d,d)
                new_raw = float(cvec.max())
                if new_raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                    cvec_check = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
                    raw_check = float(cvec_check.max())
                    if raw_check < best_raw - 1e-10:
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_raw = raw_check
                        best_c = raw_check*dx
                        cvec = cvec_check
                        improved=True
                        break
                    else:
                        apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,d,-d)
                        cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
                else:
                    apply_pair_move(h,cvec,s,t,d,-d)
                actions += 1
                if actions % 500 == 0:
                    cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h,n)
                    # h is still best_h if no improvement; ensure best_raw?
                    cur_raw = float(cvec.max())
                    if cur_raw != best_raw: # due drift
                        best_raw = cur_raw  # but h=best_h? If h=best_h, should be same. If not? no.
            if improved: break
        if not improved:
            break
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```
If exact verification fails, h should be best_h after revert. We recompute cvec. Good.

```
sh = alpha*(vals - m)
sh_max = float(sh.max())
if not np.isfinite(sh_max): sh_max=0.0
sh = sh - sh_max
w = np.exp(sh)
sw = float(w.sum())
if sw > 1e-300:
    lse = sh_max + float(np.log(sw))
else:
    # fallback: use close count
    cnt = int(np.sum(vals >= m - max(1e-12, 20.0/alpha)))
    if cnt <=0: lse = alpha*0.0? sw?; w = argmax
    else: lse = sh_max + float(np.log(cnt))
val = m + lse/alpha
```
If underflow, sh_max=0, lse~log(cnt). weights fallback uniform. Good.

```
def softmax_info(vals, alpha):
    if vals.size == 0: return 0.0, np.zeros(0), 0.0
    m = float(vals.max())
    if alpha <= 0:
       w = np.ones(vals.size, dtype=float)/vals.size
       lse = 0.0
       return m, w, lse
    sh = alpha*(vals - m)
    sh_max = float(sh.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sh_max): sh_max=0.0
    sh = sh - sh_max
    w = np.exp(sh)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw > 1e-300:
       lse = sh_max + float(np.log(sw))
       w = w / sw
    else:
       tol = max(1e-12, 20.0/alpha)
       close = vals >= m - tol
       cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(close))
       if cnt <=0:
          idx = int(np.argmax(vals))
          w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float); w[idx]=1.0
          lse = sh_max
       else:
          w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float); w[close]=1.0/cnt
          lse = sh_max + float(np.log(cnt))
    return m, w, lse
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c_vec_raw(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def compute_c_vec(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return compute_c_vec_raw(h, n) * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)) * dx)


def project_feasible(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if y.size != n:
        if y.size < n:
            y = np.pad(y, (0, n - y.size), mode='edge')
        else:
            y = y[:n]
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    lo = float(np.min(y)) - float(n) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + float(n) + 1.0

    for _ in range(12):
        if float(np.clip(y - lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) >= target:
            break
        lo -= float(n)
    for _ in range(12):
        if float(np.clip(y - hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) <= target:
            break
        hi += float(n)

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    return np.clip(y - lam, 0.0, 1.0)


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_old = int(h.size)
    if n_old <= 0:
        return np.full(n_new, 0.5, dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def threshold_project(h, n, seed=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    m = int(round(target))
    if abs(target - float(m)) < 1e-12 and 0 < m < n:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(-h, kind='mergesort')[:m]
        out[order] = 1.0
        return out
    return h


def random_smooth_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(10):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 13))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.65))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        y += amp * np.cos(np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)
    y = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.tanh(y)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(y, n)


def random_binary_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=target, replace=False)
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def choose_active(c, n, tol, minact, maxact):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    maxv = float(c.max())
    idx = np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
    m = int(idx.size)
    if m <= 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    elif m < minact:
        if c.size <= minact:
            idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -minact)[-minact:]
    elif m > maxact:
        if c.size <= maxact:
            idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -maxact)[-maxact:]
    return idx.astype(int, copy=False)


def lbfgs_limits(n, actlen):
    if n <= 500:
        mi, mf = (260, 5000)
    elif n <= 800:
        mi, mf = (220, 3000)
    elif n <= 1100:
        mi, mf = (180, 1800)
    elif n <= 1400:
        mi, mf = (140, 1100)
    else:
        mi, mf = (110, 700)
    if actlen <= 25:
        return (2 * mi, min(mf * 3, 10000))
    if actlen <= 70:
        return (2 * mi, min(mf * 2, 8000))
    return (mi, mf)


def softmax_info(vals, alpha):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=float), 0.0
    m = float(vals.max())
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(vals.size, dtype=float) / float(vals.size)
        return m, w, 0.0
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    sh_max = float(sh.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sh_max):
        sh_max = 0.0
    sh = sh - sh_max
    w = np.exp(sh)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw > 1e-300:
        lse = sh_max + float(np.log(sw))
        w = w / sw
    else:
        tol = max(1e-12, 20.0 / alpha)
        close = vals >= (m - tol)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(close))
        if cnt <= 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(vals))
            w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            lse = sh_max
        else:
            w = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float)
            w[close] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
            lse = sh_max + float(np.log(float(cnt)))
    return m, w, lse


def mask_obj(x, n, active, ks, alpha, mu, target):
    c = compute_c_vec(x, n)
    vals = c[active]
    m, w, lse = softmax_info(vals, alpha)
    val = m + lse / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        s = float(np.sum(x)) - target
        val += mu * s * s
    return float(val)


def mask_grad(x, n, active, ks, alpha, mu, target):
    c = compute_c_vec(x, n)
    vals = c[active]
    m, w, lse = softmax_info(vals, alpha)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for pos in range(active.size):
        wi = float(w[pos])
        if wi < 1e-15:
            continue
        kk = int(ks[pos])
        wm = wi * dx
        if kk >= 0:
            nv = n - kk
            grad[:nv] += wm * (1.0 - x[kk:n])
            grad[kk:] += -wm * x[:nv]
        else:
            kk = -kk
            nv = n - kk
            grad[kk:] += wm * (1.0 - x[:nv])
            grad[:nv] += -wm * x[kk:]
    if mu != 0.0:
        grad += 2.0 * mu * (float(np.sum(x)) - target)
    return grad


def active_lbfgs(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    tols = (0.004, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 5e-5)
    alphas = (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0)
    mu = 10.0
    minact = 5
    maxact = max(20, min(240, n // 4))
    stalls = 0
    passes = 0

    while passes < 3 and time.time() < deadline:
        passes += 1
        for tol in tols:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2.0 or stalls >= 4:
                break
            for alpha in alphas:
                if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                cvec = compute_c_vec(h, n)
                active = choose_active(cvec, n, float(tol), minact, maxact)
                if active.size == 0:
                    break
                ks = active - (n - 1)
                maxiter, maxfun = lbfgs_limits(int(n), int(active.size))
                try:
                    res = minimize(
                        mask_obj,
                        h,
                        jac=mask_grad,
                        args=(int(n), active, ks, float(alpha), float(mu), float(target)),
                        method='L-BFGS-B',
                        bounds=bounds,
                        options={
                            'maxiter': int(maxiter),
                            'ftol': 1e-13,
                            'gtol': 1e-8,
                            'maxls': 8,
                            'maxfun': int(maxfun),
                            'maxcor': 10,
                        }
                    )
                    new_h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
                    new_c = compute_c5(new_h, n)
                except Exception:
                    stalls += 1
                    if stalls >= 4:
                        break
                    continue
                if new_c < best_c - 1e-13:
                    h = new_h
                    best_c = new_c
                    stalls = 0
                else:
                    stalls += 1
                if stalls >= 4:
                    break
            if stalls >= 4:
                break
    return h, best_c


def apply_pair_move(h, c, i, j, di, dj):
    n = int(h.size)
    one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
    h_rev = h[::-1]

    c[n - 1 - i : n - 1 - i + n] += di * one_minus_h
    c[n - 1 - j : n - 1 - j + n] += dj * one_minus_h
    c[i : i + n] -= di * h_rev
    c[j : j + n] -= dj * h_rev

    r0 = n - 1
    c[r0] -= di * di + dj * dj
    r1 = n - 1 + (j - i)
    r2 = n - 1 + (i - j)
    cross = -di * dj
    if r1 == r2:
        c[r1] += cross
    else:
        c[r1] += cross
        c[r2] += cross


def candidate_score(candidate):
    return float(candidate[2])


def generate_local_candidates(h, cvec, n):
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    m = int(cvec.size)
    a_top = min(26, m)
    if a_top <= 0:
        return cands
    if m <= a_top:
        top_idx = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    else:
        top_idx = np.argpartition(cvec, -a_top)[-a_top:]
    top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(-cvec[top_idx], kind='mergesort')]

    shift_count = min(20, int(top_idx.size))
    if n > 1200:
        shift_count = min(14, shift_count)
    pair_count = 10
    max_cands = 260

    max_raw = float(cvec.max())
    zero_tol = max(1e-8, 0.005 * (0.5 * float(n)))

    for idx in top_idx[:shift_count]:
        idx = int(idx)
        k = idx - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            L = n - k
            if L < 2:
                continue
            a = h[:L]
            b = h[k:k + L]
            use_kneg = False
        else:
            kk = -k
            L = n - kk
            if L < 2:
                continue
            a = h[kk:n]
            b = h[:L]
            use_kneg = True

        total = a + b - 2.0 * a * b
        diff = np.abs(a - b)
        score = total * diff
        p_use = min(pair_count, int(L))
        if p_use <= 0:
            continue
        if p_use >= L:
            local_idx = np.argsort(-score, kind='mergesort')
        else:
            local_idx = np.argpartition(-score, p_use)[:p_use]

        for p in local_idx:
            p = int(p)
            ai = float(a[p])
            bi = float(b[p])
            d = abs(ai - bi)
            if d < 1e-5:
                continue
            if ai > bi:
                si = p
                ti = (k + p) if not use_kneg else p
                dd = d
            else:
                si = (k + p) if not use_kneg else p
                ti = p
                dd = d
            if use_kneg and ai > bi:
                # a is h[kk:]; b is h[:]
                si = kk + p
                ti = p
            elif use_kneg and bi > ai:
                si = p
                ti = kk + p
            else:
                # k >= 0
                si = p
                ti = k + p
            key = int(si) * int(n) + int(ti)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            cands.append((int(si), int(ti), float(dd)))
            if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                return cands

    if cvec.size > 0 and cvec[n - 1] >= max_raw - zero_tol:
        kgl = min(10, n - 1)
        if kgl > 0:
            lo = np.argpartition(h, kgl)[:kgl]
            hi = np.argpartition(-h, kgl)[:kgl]
            added = 0
            for ii in lo:
                ii = int(ii)
                for jj in hi:
                    jj = int(jj)
                    if ii == jj:
                        continue
                    if h[ii] < h[jj] - 1e-5:
                        dd = 0.15 * float(h[jj] - h[ii])
                        if dd > 0.04:
                            dd = 0.04
                        if dd >= 1e-4:
                            key = ii * n + jj
                            if key not in seen:
                                seen.add(key)
                                cands.append((ii, jj, float(dd)))
                                added += 1
                if added >= 30 or len(cands) >= max_cands:
                    break

    cands.sort(key=candidate_score, reverse=True)
    return cands[:max_cands]


def local_exact_search(h, cvec, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(best_h, n)
    best_raw = float(cvec.max())
    best_c = best_raw * dx

    while time.time() < deadline:
        cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)
        cur_raw = float(cvec.max())
        if cur_raw < best_raw - 1e-12:
            best_raw = cur_raw
            best_c = cur_raw * dx
            best_h = h.copy()

        cands = generate_local_candidates(h, cvec, n)
        if not cands:
            break

        improved = False
        actions = 0
        for cand in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            s, t, dd = cand
            if s == t:
                continue
            hs = float(h[s])
            ht = float(h[t])
            maxd = min(hs, 1.0 - ht)
            if maxd <= 1e-12:
                continue

            for frac in (0.5, 0.25, 1.0, 0.1):
                d = frac * dd
                if d > maxd:
                    d = maxd
                if d <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                apply_pair_move(h, cvec, s, t, -float(d), float(d))
                new_raw = float(cvec.max())
                if new_raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                    cvec_check = compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)
                    raw_check = float(cvec_check.max())
                    if raw_check < best_raw - 1e-10:
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_raw = raw_check
                        best_c = raw_check * dx
                        cvec = cvec_check
                        improved = True
                        break
                    else:
                        apply_pair_move(h, cvec, s, t, float(d), -float(d))
                        cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)
                else:
                    apply_pair_move(h, cvec, s, t, float(d), -float(d))

                actions += 1
                if actions % 700 == 0:
                    cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)
                    cur_raw = float(cvec.max())
                    if cur_raw < best_raw - 1e-12:
                        best_raw = cur_raw
                        best_c = cur_raw * dx
                        best_h = h.copy()
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def improve_candidate(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    cycles = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and cycles < 5:
        cycles += 1
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 18.0:
            break

        if rem > 45.0:
            if n <= 700:
                lb_cap = 120.0
            elif n <= 1000:
                lb_cap = 100.0
            elif n <= 1300:
                lb_cap = 85.0
            else:
                lb_cap = 70.0
            lb_frac = 0.45 if cycles == 1 else 0.35
            lb_alloc = min(rem * lb_frac, lb_cap)
            lb_alloc = max(15.0, lb_alloc)
            lb_dl = min(deadline - 12.0, time.time() + lb_alloc)
            if lb_dl > time.time() + 4.0:
                h, c_tmp = active_lbfgs(h, n, lb_dl)
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c_tmp = compute_c5(h, n)
                if c_tmp < best_c:
                    best_c = c_tmp
                    best_h = h.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        loc_alloc = min(rem * 0.7, 180.0)
        if rem < 45.0:
            loc_alloc = max(6.0, rem - 2.0)
        loc_alloc = max(8.0, loc_alloc)
        loc_dl = min(deadline - 1.0, time.time() + loc_alloc)

        cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(h, n)
        h, c_tmp = local_exact_search(h, cvec, n, loc_dl)
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c_tmp = compute_c5(h, n)
        if c_tmp < best_c:
            best_c = c_tmp
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1100.0
    if budget < 30.0:
        budget = 30.0
    deadline = t0 + budget - 6.0

    init = None
    try:
        init = initial_h_values
    except Exception:
        init = None

    if init is not None:
        h0 = np.asarray(init, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if h0.size <= 1:
            h0 = None
    else:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None:
        n0 = 500
        h0 = random_smooth_start(n0, int(seed))
    else:
        n0 = int(h0.size)
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = int(n0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 12.0:
        parent_alloc = 50.0 if budget >= 180.0 else max(5.0, deadline - time.time() - 5.0)
        parent_dl = min(deadline - 6.0, time.time() + parent_alloc)
        if parent_dl > time.time() + 5.0:
            h_try, c_try = improve_candidate(best_h, best_n, parent_dl)
            if c_try < best_c:
                best_c = c_try
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                best_n = int(h_try.size)

    tasks = [
        (600, 80.0, 'main'),
        (900, 120.0, 'main'),
        (1100, 300.0, 'main'),
        (1400, 200.0, 'main'),
        (1100, 80.0, 'threshold'),
        (1400, 60.0, 'threshold'),
        (900, 50.0, 'random'),
    ]

    for task_n, alloc, kind in tasks:
        task_n = int(task_n)
        if task_n < 100:
            continue
        if time.time() > deadline - 55.0:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        a = min(float(alloc), rem - 25.0)
        if a < 25.0:
            a = min(rem, 30.0)
            if a < 18.0:
                break

        if kind == 'main':
            start = resample_h(best_h, best_n, task_n)
        elif kind == 'threshold':
            base = resample_h(best_h, best_n, task_n)
            start = threshold_project(base, task_n, int(seed) + task_n)
        else:
            start = random_smooth_start(task_n, int(seed * 1000 + task_n))

        dl = time.time() + a
        if dl > deadline:
            dl = deadline
        h_try, c_try = improve_candidate(start, task_n, dl)
        if c_try < best_c:
            best_c = c_try
            best_h = h_try.copy()
            best_n = int(h_try.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        n_down = max(200, best_n // 2)
        if n_down != best_n and n_down <= best_n:
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            a = min(70.0, rem - 10.0)
            if a > 25.0:
                start = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_down)
                h_try, c_try = improve_candidate(start, n_down, time.time() + a)
                if c_try < best_c:
                    best_c = c_try
                    best_h = h_try.copy()
                    best_n = int(h_try.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        a = min(55.0, rem - 10.0)
        if a > 20.0:
            start = random_smooth_start(best_n, int(seed * 7 + 12345))
            h_try, c_try = improve_candidate(start, best_n, time.time() + a)
            if c_try < best_c:
                best_c = c_try
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                best_n = int(h_try.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        cvec = compute_c_vec_raw(best_h, best_n)
        h_try, c_try = local_exact_search(best_h, cvec, best_n, deadline - 1.0)
        h_try = project_feasible(h_try, best_n)
        c_try = compute_c5(h_try, best_n)
        if c_try < best_c:
            best_c = c_try
            best_h = h_try.copy()
            best_n = int(h_try.size)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
